  2. With serve stopped (the swtpm serves one connection; the state lock is free): `ca-rotate prepare --role user --out <file>` through `env.signerCmd`, without `--key`: exit 0, the first line `role user generation 2 <fp2> ecdsa-sha2-nistp256 vtpm`, no `location:` line, and `tpm/user-g2.tpmkey` and `tpm/user-g2.auth` regular files with mode 0600 below the state directory.
  3. Ceremony 1 with a `tpmRotCeremony` helper built like caRotCeremony but through `keyrosterWithAgent(t, env.RootAgent, ...)` for `root sign` and `keyrosterWithAgent(t, "", ...)` for `trust verify --prev`: `--prev` the genesis bundle directory (`env.BundleDir`, its SHA-256 from its `bundle.json`), `--roots` the roots.pub in `env.BundleDir`'s parent, the root fingerprint `env.RootFingerprints[0]`, `--ca-next user=<file> --ca-next-fingerprint user=<fp2>`; the display names `CA user gen 2 <fp2>: none -> next`. Install it through `env.signerCmd` (`installed bundle version 2`).
  4. `export := env.exportLog(t)`. Ceremony 2 from ceremony 1's output directory with `--ca-switch user --drain-allowance 60s`: the display names `CA user gen 1 <fp1>: active -> draining, trusted until` and `CA user gen 2 <fp2>: next -> active`. Install it with `--previous <export>`: the output names `drain user generation 1 until`.
  5. `serve` through `env.signerCmd` exits non-zero naming `draining_key_still_usable`, and no socket appears; doctor, while the key is still held, prints `FAIL draining_key_still_usable: user generation 1 <fp1> is still held by the backend`.
  6. `ca-rotate retire-key --role user` through `env.signerCmd`: exit 0, `role user generation 1 <fp1>: removed` and `retire-key: no draining key of role user is held`; `tpm/user.tpmkey` and `tpm/user.auth` are gone, and `tpm/user-g2.*`, `tpm/host.*`, `tpm/machine.*`, `tpm/ops.*` and `tpm/log.*` are all there; a second run exits 0 with `role user generation 1 <fp1>: not held`. Then doctor prints no line holding `draining_key_`.
  7. Serve again; issue and log in through the parameterised helper with generation 2's public key (written from the prepare file into a `.pub` file): the certificate's `Signing CA` is fp2, and sshd trusts fp2's key only. Stop serve.
  8. `tpmAuditVerify(t, env, 8, 2)`: 8 entries derived from the leaves the test writes (ca_init, bundle_install of v1, the first start's krl_publish, the generation-1 issuance, ca_generation, bundle_install of v2 and of v3, the generation-2 issuance; TestTPMRestartPersistence shows that a later start under an hour publishes no KRL) and 2 issuances. The local run below confirms the count; if it reads another number, the executor lists the export's leaf kinds in the SUMMARY and sets the count only to what that list explains, never to a number it cannot explain.
  9. The test logs one fixed line, `tpm rotation: generation 2 signs and generation 1's key and auth files are gone`.

Save the staged diff of the file to a file outside the repository and read all of it, every added, removed and context line, with no `--stat` and no truncation; run the whole-file legs of the publish scan (the seventh verify command's first loop) on both plan files, then commit. No later step edits this file.

**The split's bookkeeping, one second docs commit (B6)** (`docs(03-12f2): add the host-CA end-to-end plan and shift the later waves`), made before the PR is opened, after a full read of `git diff --cached` of every staged file, one file at a time (it edits six planning files by scoped edits, where a skimmed diff hides an extra changed line):

No other line changes here.

**Gates.** Run the test-time delta gate (Budget) and the full gate. If this session or WSL boot is not the one that measured the base (after a continuation it never is), re-measure the base on origin/main in a detached worktree first, in place. `scripts/capslock-check.sh` is expected to report no new pair; a new pair stops the plan.

**Push, the PR, the lanes, the tracking update and the pre-merge review.**
1. Before every publish commit, run the publish scan (the seventh verify command) on the two plan files and every added line. That command reads committed history only, so before each commit after P6 and B6 (the tracking-and-SUMMARY commit of step 4 and every fix-round commit) also stage the files by path, the SUMMARY included, and scan the staged added lines with `( D=$(set -o pipefail; git diff -U0 --cached | grep '^+' | grep -v '^+++') && test -n "$D" && T='find[i]ng|FIX[-]PLAN|security[-]audit[-]skill|scratch[p]ad|App[D]ata|run[-]1|([0-9]{1,3}\.){3}[0-9]{1,3}' && CS=$(mktemp) && CI=$(mktemp) && trap 'rm -f "$CS" "$CI"' EXIT && grep -v '^[[:space:]]*#' "$OWNER_DENY_CS" | grep . > "$CS" && grep -v '^[[:space:]]*#' "$OWNER_DENY_CI" | grep . > "$CI" && ! printf '%s\n' "$D" | grep -iE "$T" | grep -vE '127\.0\.0\.[0-9]+' | grep -q . && ! printf '%s\n' "$D" | grep -qE -f "$CS" && ! printf '%s\n' "$D" | grep -qiE -f "$CI" )`, which must exit 0 before the commit and the push; the seventh verify command stays as it is. Commit by path only, signed and authored as Daniel. Push with `env -u GITHUB_TOKEN -u GH_TOKEN git push -u origin p03/12f-ca-rotation-tpm-piv-generations`.
2. The PR (`scripts/gh-as-bot.sh pr create --title "feat(signer): rotate CA keys on the TPM and PIV backends"`). The body is neutral, lists the changes as in the objective and names the limits that remain, each on one line:
   - TPM generations (`tpm/<role>-g<N>.tpmkey` and `.auth`, noDA), exact addressing, presence by lstat of either file, and removal of a regular key file with its auth file after a fingerprint check (a link, another entry or a lone auth file at the generation's name is unlinked unchecked, because no key at that name can be opened); doctor checks a draining TPM generation by its files and warns about unrecorded ones;
   - PIV generations in the first slot of 0x87 to 0x95 that no row records, never reused, `piv_no_free_slot`, addressing and presence by the recorded slot's key, and removal by overwriting the draining slot with a throwaway key on every firmware (the pinned piv-go has no delete), with the management key on the host for the run only; the backend reads that key only when it writes to the card, so serve, install-bundle and doctor run with it off the host; a key in the next generation slot that no row records stops later PIV prepares until the slot is emptied, as the runbooks say;
   - the fake card moved into a `pivfake`-tagged file behind an open hook, and build-piv's proof that the release piv binary holds none;
   - the backend gate lifted for tpm and piv; the command-level legs on swtpm and on the fake card, run by new steps of the existing e2e-tpm and build-piv jobs with a PASS check per test; TestTPMCARotation;
   - the per-backend runbook text, the move-aside sentence before ceremony 1's export, and their pins;
   - this plan's text (replacing the early text on main), 03-12f2's early text and the one wave shift (39/44 in 43 waves);
   - no new required check: the ruleset still lists 30, and no job was added or renamed;
   - UNVERIFIED, plainly: every physical TPM, the homelab vTPM signer (its first prepare is 03-15's), every YubiKey (generation slots, the overwrite destroying the old key, PIN once across processes, every `ykman` step the runbooks name), deletion on firmware 5.7 and later (not implemented), and the host CA end to end (03-12f2).
   Do not enable auto-merge, now or later.
3. **The tracking update, once, now that the PR exists.** ROADMAP.md: the Plans line `38/43 plans executed in 42 waves` becomes `39/44 plans executed in 43 waves`, the progress row `| 38/43 |` becomes `| 39/44 |`, and this plan's row is ticked. STATE.md, by GSD's tracking update (`state.advance-plan`, `state.update-progress`, `roadmap.update-plan-progress`, which reset the counts from the files): `total_plans` goes from 78 to 79 (03-12f2's file is the 79th), `completed_plans` from 73 to 74, and `Plan: 38 of 43` becomes `Plan: 39 of 44` (the commands bump only the leading number, and they write neither `in 43 waves` nor `of 44`, as 03-12e found: those are scoped edits). Then one scoped edit (`Edit`, never a whole-file write) replaces the single line `stopped_at: "Completed 03-12e2-PLAN.md (PR #111)"` with `stopped_at: "Completed 03-12f-PLAN.md (PR #N)"`, N being this plan's PR (`scripts/gh-as-bot.sh pr view p03/12f-ca-rotation-tpm-piv-generations --json number --jq .number`).
4. Check that GSD's roadmap update ticked only this plan's row: eight rows start with `03-12` after B6 (03-12 to 03-12e2, this plan and 03-12f2), and `03-12f` is a prefix of `03-12f2`, so the hazard is real. Every other Phase 3 row must be as B6 left it. If another row was ticked, untick it with a scoped edit, record it in the SUMMARY and rerun the ROADMAP gate (the third verify command). Then write the SUMMARY as the paragraph below says, with its `Pre-merge review` section marked pending; stage by path, read the staged diffs in full, run step 1's staged-line scan; commit `docs(03-12f): complete ca-rotation-tpm-piv-generations plan` and push.
5. Wait for the required checks, run the house e2e gate in `MODE=pr` for HEAD (the first verify command) and the lane gate of e2e-tpm and build-piv (the second): every named PASS line in the PR run's logs, no skipped leg, and the release guard's line.
6. The orchestrator then runs the pre-merge review on the green PR, before Task 7, and Task 7 does not start until the review is recorded or the owner says to skip it. The review is lean by default: one lens on the fail-closed table row by row (TPM and PIV Holds, removal, addressing, the allocator: can any error, any unparsable location or any generation-2 `""` give "not held" or a write; can RemoveGeneration write a slot whose key it did not just read and match?), one lens on the seam and the lanes (can a release binary contain the fake; does every TPM and PIV test run in a lane that fails when it skips; are swtpm's one connection and the fake's exclusive open respected?), one lens on the runbooks and docs (every claim named with its CI test or UNVERIFIED, the management key's round trip, no root write below a directory the signer's user owns, the move-aside sentence) and, at the orchestrator's choice, one Codex lens on `git diff origin/main...HEAD` and the PR's text only, never a ceremony folder, a passphrase file, `~/.ssh` or any planning file outside the repository. It also checks the diff against the owner's pattern files and reads every committed file in full. Its results, the fix rounds and their checks are recorded in the SUMMARY under `Pre-merge review`; if the owner merges first, the SUMMARY says that the review ran after the merge or did not run.

**The SUMMARY** contains: the PR number and the IDs of the CI runs that exist when it is written (a run for the SUMMARY's own commit cannot be named in it, 03-12e's deviation 29; the review's docs commit adds the head it reviewed); both plan files' sizes and sha256 lines; BASE and the re-read of the ledger's anchors with every difference; R1, T1, S2, R2, T2, R3, T3, G4, R5, W5, P6 and B6 with each RED, the cases that passed on R and why, every declaration an R commit added, and the statement, per commit, that `git diff --cached` was read in full after the last edit; every mutant (M175 to M179, M339 to M351, M353 to M360 and M363 to M367, M176 as its two applications, and the command-level re-runs of M177 to M179 and M363) under the mutant rule, with the sha256 and `git status` records; the release guard's negative proof with its four exit codes and each leg's stderr line; the `test-time base:` line and the delta gate's readings; the capslock pair counts after T1 and T3; Task 4's local run and the PR's e2e-tpm and build-piv run IDs with each lane's duration (the e2e-tpm run ID is the CI evidence 03-16 names if its rotation drill is skipped); the totals after the tracking update (`39/44 plans executed in 43 waves`, `| 39/44 |`, `total_plans: 79`, `completed_plans: 74`, `Plan: 39 of 44` and the new `stopped_at`); B6's exact edits; the threat-ID and mutant-number checks and the publish scan; the pre-existing items left as they are (the two test/e2e lint lines of the carry-in and piv.md step 3's `sudo install` lines); what stays UNVERIFIED, in the words of this plan; the pre-merge review; `requirements-completed` left empty: KEY-08 completes in 03-13.

## Cross-plan notes

- **03-12f2 (wave 39, depends on this plan).** This PR publishes its early text and the wave shift. It takes TestHostCARotation, the host-CA section of ca-rotation.md, the enroll-user.md paragraph, the e2e-rotation lane's `-run` list and PASS loop and CONTRIBUTING's lane rows, and it shrinks `no_plan_numbers`'s allowlist from `03-12f2` to `03-13` and `03-16` once its own text names no 03-12f2. This plan leaves it: ca-rotation.md naming 03-12f2 twice (the host-CA section and "Run in CI"), the allowlist holding `03-12f2`, and e2e.yml's comment naming 03-12f2. It edits nothing 03-12f2 changes in test/e2e (ca_rotation_test.go stays as it is).
- **03-13 (wave 40 after the shift, depends on this plan).** Its files_modified (03-13-PLAN.md:7-28) lists no keystore file, so this plan makes TPM and PIV generations work for every role: TPM file names come from all five roles (tpm.go:121) and the PIV pool is shared by every role's later generations, ops and log included. Its test `prepare_log_and_ops_use_generation_allocator` (03-13-PLAN.md:154) runs "with 03-12's fake PIV card" at command level; that is this plan's seam: in a `piv,pivfake` build, `keystore.Open("piv", ...)` opens the process-wide fake card of the serial named in the options, and `FakePIN`, `FakeManagementKeyHex` and `FakeCardGenerated` are exported for such a test. 03-13's files_modified lists no cmd/keyroster-signer test, no file tagged `piv && pivfake` and not piv.yml, and a test in untagged internal/signer cannot reach the fake, so 03-13's reconcile must add what a command-level use needs: a cmd/keyroster-signer test in the hook-and-skip pattern of this plan's PIV leg (a variable in rotate_test.go and a `linux && piv && pivfake` file that sets it), a build-piv `-run` with its indented PASS check (piv.yml in its files_modified), and one fake-card serial per test (the registry is process-wide per serial with no reset, so a reused serial, or a `-count` above 1, finds the slots occupied). 03-13-PLAN.md:127 and :289 also say that the allocator never overwrites a slot, without the one exception (retire-key overwrites the draining generation's own slot); that is true of prepare, and 03-13's reconcile states the exception. 03-13-PLAN.md:127 says both that the new log and ops keys open with `Key(role, fingerprint)` and that they open with KeyFor; on TPM and PIV only `KeyFor` (through KeyAt) opens a generation of 2 or later, so 03-13's reconcile settles it. This plan does not edit 03-13's text beyond its `wave:` line.
- **03-15 (wave 42).** Its prepare on the homelab TPM signer (03-15-PLAN.md:183) uses this plan's TPM generations; the signer-install.md text says tpm needs no `--key`. The homelab vTPM stays UNVERIFIED until it runs.
- **03-16 (wave 43).** Its retire-key on the homelab signer (03-16-PLAN.md:252, "TPM: it removes the key and auth files") matches this plan's output; if its rotation drill is skipped, the CI evidence it names is this PR's e2e-tpm run ID, recorded in the SUMMARY.
- **03-12e (merged).** Its doctor WARN for "any backend that is neither software nor agent (tpm and piv cannot hold a generation of a draining entry before 03-12f)" (03-12e-SUMMARY.md) ends here for TPM; PIV keeps it by design. Its `keystore.Holds`, `GenerationRemover` and `RetireKeys` are extended to TPM and PIV as 03-12e-PLAN.md:830 handed over.
- **Threat legs.** T-03-245's TPM and PIV restart leg (a signer that installs a rotated bundle and cannot restart; defined in 03-12-PLAN.md:769) is this plan's TestSignerRestartMixedBundle `tpm` and `piv` legs. T-03-424's TPM and PIV removal leg and its residual text (defined in 03-12e-PLAN.md:995) are Tasks 1 to 3 and 5. T-03-419's planted-key residual (03-12-PLAN.md:770) covers a placed TPM key for `--key`; T-03-464 adds its noDA side. Each ID is defined only in the plan named in its register.

## Edge Coverage (spec-less fallback, this plan)

**Flagged assumptions.**
- **Real hardware is not exercised** (owner rule): swtpm stands in for every TPM and the fake card for every YubiKey; "What stays UNVERIFIED" lists what that leaves out.
- **The fake card's semantics follow piv-go as documented**, not a card: an empty slot reads `ErrNotFound`, GenerateKey replaces a key, a second open is refused, KeyInfo needs no PIN (the backend always holds a PIN-verified session, piv.go:164, so the last is moot for serve and retire-key).
- **swtpm serves one connection at a time**, read in swtpm's source, not in its documentation; every TPM test follows it.

**Edges:**
- **TPM:** generation 0, 1 and N; all five roles; the key or auth file present alone; a link, a dangling link and a directory at a name; the key directory replaced by a file, at 0750 or a link; another key or garbage at the name; a sync failure on provision and on removal; a crash between the unlinks; a missing generation never falling back; a placed key without noDA; generation 1 removed while generation 2 works; a reopen.
- **PIV:** firmware 5.4.3 and 5.7.0; 0, 14 and 15 recorded slots; an emptied and an overwritten recorded slot; an unrecorded occupied candidate; malformed and duplicate `used` entries; the genesis slot of generation 1 and every generation slot; `""` for a later generation; another key, an empty slot and a read error at the recorded slot; no management key; the management-key file absent or refused at open (opens) and at a card write (refused before the write), and a well-formed wrong key; a lost response after the card acted; ops and log sharing the pool; `--key` refused by the resolver; an unrecorded key in the next slot refused again on a second prepare.
- **Commands and lanes:** the gate for tpm, piv, an unknown name and `""`; prepare's `location:` line; serve's refusal; retire-key's first and second run; doctor per backend; a skipped leg failing the lane; the fake absent from the release binary.
- **Runbooks:** the move-aside sentence before each writing block, never in the previous section or after the block; no plan number in prose but 03-12f2, 03-13 and 03-16; every per-backend phrase in its own bullet's window; the management key's round trip as one block, run as the signer's user, with the shred on every exit status and before the start; the restore runbook's own checks.
- **Controls that must not change:** the software and agent legs and every 03-12 to 03-12e2 rotation test, build-piv's default-build exclusion, the custody banner, ceremony 1's install block, and the e2e-rotation lane.

<threat_model>
## Trust Boundaries

| Boundary | Description |
|----------|-------------|
| the signer → the TPM or the card | generation keys live in the TPM's wrapped files or the card's slots and must be addressed, checked and removed by generation and key, never by number or location alone |
| signer.db → the backends | the location column is unlogged and unconstrained, so an edit of it must never decide which slot is written |
| the build → the release binary | test code (the fake card) must never reach a binary an operator runs |
| the runbook → the operator | the management key's round trip and the root-run commands on the signer host |

## STRIDE Threat Register

Threat IDs are unique within a phase. Task 1's precondition checks that no register row on origin/main defines T-03-246 or T-03-461 to T-03-464 other than this plan's own; the other IDs this plan carries a leg of are in "Cross-plan notes".

| Threat ID | Category | Component | Severity | Disposition | Mitigation Plan |
|-----------|----------|-----------|----------|-------------|-----------------|
| T-03-246 | Denial of service | PIV slots used up, or PIV rotation blocked by a key in a generation slot that no row records | low | mitigate (partial) | `piv_no_free_slot` before any card write; the allocator goes by generation rows, so each retired slot stays consumed: 15 slots shared with 03-13's ops and log generations, a limit the runbook states (TestPIVGenerationAllocator, M175, M176). An occupied candidate that no row records is refused `ErrSlotOccupied` with a pointer to piv.md, never skipped or overwritten (M176). Residual: such a key (a lost prepare, a signer.db restored from before a prepare, a key put there by hand) blocks every later PIV prepare for every role until the slot is emptied; overwriting the slot by hand does not clear it; on firmware 5.7 and later `ykman piv keys delete <slot>` empties it when the rule of piv.md "Generation slots" allows it (never on a restored database before its restore section has run to its end, never while a signed bundle waits to be installed, never a key kept there for another use); below 5.7 nothing empties a slot short of `ykman piv reset`, which destroys every CA key on the card; every `ykman` command is UNVERIFIED. Adopting such a key by its fingerprint is a later hardening item |
| T-03-461 | Tampering | an edited, unlogged PIV location redirects a removal or makes Holds answer "not held" | medium | mitigate (partial) | the resolver accepts only `""` for generation 1 and only 0x87 to 0x95 for later generations (M346, M356), and RemoveGeneration writes only after the slot's key matches the draining fingerprint (M345, M357), so an edit can never destroy the active, ops or log key. Residual: an edit that points a draining row at any slot that does not hold the draining key (an empty slot, a later generation's slot, or a slot an earlier retire-key overwrote) makes Holds and serve answer "not held" while the key stays in its real slot; that needs database write access on the signer host, where the PIN file is also readable; detection only, and audit cannot show the slot because the location is not logged |
| T-03-462 | Elevation of privilege | the PIV management key back on the signer host for prepare and retire-key | medium | mitigate (partial) | the backend reads the management key only at the start of a card write (Provision, ProvisionGeneration, and RemoveGeneration after it read the slot and found the draining key), never at open, so serve, install-bundle and doctor run with the file absent and serve never holds the key in memory, also when the file is present (M363); ProvisionGeneration and RemoveGeneration refuse before any card write (RemoveGeneration reads the slot's metadata first) without the option or with a missing or refused file, naming `mgmt-key-file` (M348, M364); piv.md step 5's block places it for the run only, as the signer's user, and shreds it on every exit status of the command, before the signer starts (M367). Residual: whoever reads it while it is on the host can overwrite or replace any slot (owner, 2026-11-20); `shred` does not reliably erase on an SSD or a copy-on-write filesystem; when the block's last line did not run to its end (an interrupt, a lost session, a failed shred) or the operator stopped after placing the key, the key stays on the host, so the runbook says to shred it by hand, by its full path, before anything else |
| T-03-463 | Tampering | test code (the fake card) in a release piv binary | medium | mitigate | fake.go compiles only with `piv && pivfake`; build-piv proves on every PR that the release build's file list excludes it, that its build info names `-tags=piv` exactly and that the binary holds no fake symbol: the file-list and symbol checks each with a positive control; the build-info check compares with the literal `-tags=piv` and fails closed when the line is missing. Residual: someone who builds with `pivfake` on purpose gets a signer whose keys live in process memory and vanish at exit; its build info shows the tag, and piv.md tells the operator to check `-tags=piv`; its Describe reports custody piv and firmware 5.7.0 like a card, so only the build info shows that the card is fake. The guard reads CI's unstripped build; on an operator's own stripped build (`-s -w`) the file-list and build-info checks still work and the symbol check stops at its positive control |
| T-03-464 | Denial of service | a TPM generation key placed for `--key` without noDA pushes the TPM-wide dictionary-attack counter into lockout | low | mitigate | KeyAt refuses a generation-2-or-later key without noDA at every open (M353); every key the backend creates has noDA (M342) |
| T-03-SC | Tampering | dependencies | low | mitigate | no module added (piv-go stays v2.6.0, go.mod and go.sum unchanged, checked in Task 6) and no package-manager install by an executor (swtpm and libpcsclite-dev are the owner's Ubuntu-archive packages, the ones CI installs, e2e-tpm.yml:49 and piv.yml:47); capslock is checked after T1 and T3 and predicted unchanged |
</threat_model>

- A PIV slot that retire-key overwrote, or that was emptied by hand, is never allocated again, and no write ever lands on a slot whose key was not just matched.
- No release binary contains the fake card; no ruleset, workflow job name, capslock baseline, module or interface of keystore.go changes; 03-12f2's early text and the one wave shift are on main.

Every row was read in the read-only checkout of 83f660c with cat, sed, grep and `git -C`; nothing there was fetched, checked out, built or run, except one `go doc` that wrote go.sum, which was restored at once with `git restore` (see "Lessons"). ## Lessons from 03-12e, 03-12e2 and PR #112, and where this plan applies them

- **Run every gate against the base tree (03-12e2, deviation 10).** "Gate base values at 83f660c" records what each count reads on main, and every count leg states its base value in its `fails_when`.
- **A prefix is not a number.** `03-12f` is a prefix of `03-12f2`; every count of the plan number uses the boundary form or the test's own regex, never a bare grep.
- **A guard's BASE must survive the commits that land before it (PR #112).** BASE is fixed at 83f660c, not derived from the predecessor's SUMMARY, because PR #112 changed docs after 2ed959f.
- **A split's sibling plan is placed at dispatch (PR #110), and both texts are checked by hash.** Two plan files here: this one replaces main's early text, 03-12f2's is new.
- **A skipped test is not a passed test.** `KEYROSTER_TPM_REQUIRE=1` locally and a PASS-line loop per named test in every new CI step; a tag change that would leave a package with no test files is caught by the loop.
- **A stub that refuses everything passes every refusal test.** Each refusal leaf runs its positive control first, so the RED lists name refusal leaves honestly.
- **Test code must not reach a release binary, and the proof must read the binary.** testonly-firewall sees whole packages only; the release guard reads the build's file list, the binary's build info and its symbols, each with a positive control.
- **A shared device has one door.** swtpm serves one connection and a card one session; the command-level legs close each backend before the next open.
- **A read-only checkout stays read-only.** While this plan was written, `go doc` with a module flag added lines to go.sum in the read-only checkout; they were restored at once and the tree checked clean. Executors and planners run `go doc` and `go list` without `GOFLAGS=-mod=mod` and check `git status` after any go command in a read-only tree.
- **CI lints three platforms and the tagged code (03-12e, deviation 32).** Every gate runs golangci-lint for linux, windows and darwin, and under `--build-tags piv,pivfake` and `e2e_tpm` where this plan adds tagged code.
- **The staged-diff read is of `git diff --cached`, after the last edit (03-12e2, deviation 6).**
